Ben had a big cup. One windy day Ben went to the park with Finn. A dog came to play with Ben and Finn. Then the dog ran away with the cup. Ben felt upset. They asked Finn, and Finn knew what to do. Ben had 5 cakes. Then Ben found 1 more. Now Ben has 6 cakes. Ben and Finn were proud. Sharing makes play more fun.
Ruby had a big book. One cold day Ruby went to the farm with Leo. A bird came to play with Ruby and Leo. Then the book was stuck in a tree. Ruby felt cross. They asked Leo, and Leo knew what to do. Ruby and Leo were proud.
Ben had a shiny book. Ben also had a bird called Kivod. One rainy day Ben went to the farm with Kai. Then the book fell into the water. Ben felt cross. Kivod ran to Ben. Kai found a long stick and helped get it back. Ben had 2 pears. Then Ben found 1 more. Now Ben has 3 pears. Ben and Kai were happy. Ben gave Kivod a hug.

Zupim ran to Kai. Kai tried again and again until it worked. Kai and Finn were proud. Kai gave Zupim a hug. It is good to keep trying.

Lily had a small drum. Lily also had a frog called Teva. One rainy day Lily went to the garden with Noah. Then the goat ran away with the drum. Lily felt sad. Teva ran to Lily. Lily tried again and again until it worked. Lily had 1 nuts. Then Lily found 4 more. Now Lily has 4 nuts. Lily and Noah were proud. Lily gave Teva a hug. It is good to keep trying.

Noah had a green book. One windy day Noah went to the town with Max. A cat came to play with Noah and Max. Then the book was stuck in a tree. Noah felt cross. Max had a good idea and they fixed it together. Noah and Max were glad.
Leo had a big hat. One sunny day Leo went to the river with Mia. A cat came to play with Leo and Mia. Then the hat fell into the water. Leo felt upset. Mia found a long stick and helped get it back. Leo and Mia were proud.
Noah had a yellow boat. Noah also had a dog called Kina. One sunny day Noah went to the beach with Lily. A dog came to play with Noah and Lily. Then the dog ran away with the boat. Noah felt sad. Kina ran to Noah. They asked Lily, and Lily knew what to do. Noah had 2 cakes. Then Noah found 1 more. Now Noah has 8 cakes. Noah and Lily were proud. Noah gave Kina a hug. Friends help each other.

Ruby had a shiny book. One cold day Ruby went to the park with Tom. A bear came to play with Ruby and Tom. Then the book was stuck in a tree. Ruby felt upset. Ruby tried again and again until it worked. Ruby had 2 apples. Then Ruby found 3 more. Now Ruby has 5 apples. Ruby and Tom were glad. It is good to keep trying.

Ben had a red boat. Ben also had a rabbit called Nelep. One warm day Ben went to the park with Kai. A goat came to play with Ben and Kai. Then the boat fell into the water. Ben felt worried. Nelep ran to Ben. Ben tried again and again until it worked. Ben had 2 nuts. Then Ben found 1 more. Now Ben has 3 nuts. Ben and Kai were glad. Ben gave Nelep a hug. Friends help each other.

Ruby had a small kite. Ruby also had a frog called Bive. One rainy day Ruby went to the garden with Leo. Then the kite broke into two pieces. Ruby felt cross. Bive ran to Ruby. They asked Leo, and Leo knew what to do. Ruby had 3 eggs. Then Ruby found 2 more. Now Ruby has 9 eggs. Ruby and Leo were happy. Ruby gave Bive a hug. Being kind is the best thing.
